In two exposure experiments, both readouts improved overall as more frames retained the original mapping. We also descriptively follow one Phase 2 trajectory after the switch to the online GMM. These results show that the numerical probability structure of the soft target does not fully determine the learned Encoder representation. The mapping of non-maximal probabilities to GMM components also matters.

</details>


### [301] [Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098)
*Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Yong Yan,Shiyuan Feng,Hanlin Wu,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个受控的多教师on-policy蒸馏（M-OPD）基准，发现标准M-OPD仅能恢复35.6%的能力headroom，归因于token级优化预算的严重错配而非梯度冲突，并提出Open-MOPD框架（令牌份额平衡、感知差距的动态预算分配、学生奖励刷新），将headroom恢复提升至83.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师on-policy蒸馏虽在实践中取得成功，但其优化动力学机制尚不明确，且缺乏开放、可严格复现的实现配方。作者希望隔离路由歧义，以受控的方式深入理解多教师能力整合失败的根源。

Method: 在SmolLM3-3B-Base上构建受控M-OPD基准（使用oracle路由隔离能力整合与路由歧义）；分析发现失败源于token级优化预算错配，由三个正交因素驱动：跨域序列长度差异、非均匀学习率导致的动态收敛漂移、异步策略更新导致的多人步奖励陈旧。提出Open-MOPD框架，包含token-share平衡、gap-aware动态预算分配和学生奖励刷新三个机制。

Result: 标准M-OPD仅捕获35.6%的可用headroom，其中指令跟随等简洁任务严重退化并过早停滞；Open-MOPD将headroom恢复从35.6%提升至83.4%，在单个可部署学生模型中系统性地恢复了跨域平衡。

Conclusion: 多教师能力整合的失败并非源于梯度冲突，而是token级优化预算的错配；通过针对性的预算平衡机制可大幅提升整合效率。作者完整开源端到端后训练配方、训练轨迹和评估套件，且硬件预算具有学术可及性。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for consolidating domain-specialized reinforcement learning (RL) experts into a single generalist student via dense, token-level reward supervision. Despite its practical success, the optimization dynamics governing multi-teacher capability integration remain poorly understood, and open, rigorously reproducible recipes are conspicuously lacking. In this work, we establish a controlled M-OPD benchmark on SmolLM3-3B-Base with oracle routing, isolating capability integration from routing ambiguity. Our investigation reveals a pronounced capability integration gap: standard M-OPD captures only 35.6% of the available headroom relative to a domain-routed oracle ensemble, with concise tasks such as instruction following suffering severe degradation and premature stagnation. Crucially, we show that this failure stems not from gradient conflict, but from a severe misallocation of the token-level optimization budget. This pathology is driven by three orthogonal factors: structural sequence-length disparities across domains, dynamic convergence drift due to non-uniform learning rates, and multi-step reward staleness from asynchronous policy updates. To resolve these imbalances, we introduce Open-MOPD, a principled framework incorporating token-share balancing, gap-aware dynamic budget allocation, and student reward refresh. Together, these mechanisms systematically restore cross-domain balance, elevating headroom recovery from 35.6% to 83.4% in a single deployable student. We fully open-source our end-to-end post-training recipe, training trajectories, and evaluation suites on an academically accessible hardware budget.

</details>


### [302] [Pretraining Reusable Inference Across Views with Synthetic Task Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19115)
*Jielong Lu,Zhihao Wu,Jiajun Yu,Zhaoliang Chen,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMPLE，一种基于先验拟合的多视角上下文学习器，通过将多视角学习重塑为可复用的、任务条件化的推理过程（而非固定融合函数），在嵌入空间中构建可控的合成任务先验来生成多样的支持-查询 episode，并采用分层推理架构在视角内、跨视角及支持-查询样本间进行推理。实验表明冻结版SIMPLE无需更新推理主干即可达到竞争性性能，轻量适配器校准在多数数据集上达到领先水平，验证了多视角推理本身可以被预训练和复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练编码器虽使异构视角的表示越来越可复用，但决定视角效用并组合证据的过程仍需要为每个下游任务重新学习，导致视角相关性、互补性、可靠性和缺失性等知识被反复丢弃而非跨任务迁移。作者希望将多视角推理本身作为可迁移的知识进行预训练和复用。

Method: 将多视角学习重新定义为学习可复用的任务条件化推理过程（而非固定融合函数），提出SIMPLE（prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner）。由于现有真实数据集覆盖的视角配置和任务结构有限，作者在嵌入空间中构建可控合成任务先验，生成具有不同类别结构、共享与视角特定因子、表示几何、跨视角依赖、可靠性水平、缺失模式及分布偏移的多样化支持-查询episodes。采用分层推理架构进行视角内、跨视角及支持-查询样本间的推理。

Result: 在多视角和多组学基准上的实验表明，SIMPLE的冻结变体在无需更新推理主干的情况下即可达到竞争性性能，而轻量适配器校准在大多数评估数据集上达到领先性能。在冻结、一次性学习和缺失视角设置下的结果均支持核心假设。

Conclusion: 多视角推理本身可以被预训练并跨任务复用，轻量适配器校准在需要时提供任务特定的对齐即可，无需为每个下游任务从头重新学习视角组合策略。

Abstract: Modern pretrained encoders make representations from heterogeneous views increasingly reusable, but the procedure that determines view utility and combines evidence is still relearned for each downstream task. Consequently, knowledge about view relevance, complementarity, reliability, and missingness is repeatedly discarded rather than transferred across tasks. We therefore reformulate multi-view learning as learning a reusable, task-conditioned inference procedure rather than a fixed fusion function. Based on this perspective, we propose SIMPLE, a prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner that predicts query labels by conditioning on a small labeled support set. Since existing real-world datasets cover only a limited range of view configurations and task structures, we construct a controllable synthetic task prior in embedding space. It generates diverse support-query episodes with varying class structures, shared and view-specific factors, representation geometries, cross-view dependencies, reliability levels, missingness patterns, and distribution shifts. A hierarchical inference architecture then performs reasoning within views, across views, and across support and query samples. Experiments on multi-view and multi-omics benchmarks demonstrate that the frozen variant of SIMPLE achieves competitive performance without updating the inference backbone, while lightweight adapter calibration attains leading performance on most evaluated datasets. Together, the results under frozen, one-shot, and missing-view settings support the central hypothesis that multi-view reasoning itself can be pretrained and reused, while lightweight adapter calibration provides task-specific alignment when needed.

</details>


### [303] [Enhancing EBSD throughput of battery electrode materials using super-resolution generative adversarial networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19117)
*John Mangum,Andrew Glaws,Francois Usseglio-Viretta,Steven Spurgeon,Donal Finegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成对抗网络（SRGAN）的机器学习超分辨率框架，用于显著提升锂离子电池电极材料（NMC正极颗粒）EBSD表征的吞吐量。该框架通过学习低分辨率EBSD数据生成高分辨率图像，在2x至12x放大倍数下均优于传统插值方法，特别在保留小晶粒和维持真实晶界方面表现突出。研究发现5倍放大（对应25倍采集速度提升或视野扩大）在晶粒尺寸和形状等关键指标上保持可接受的精度，使EBSD能够作为高通量表征工具用于材料研究和工业过程开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBSD是锂离子电池电极微观结构定量表征的关键手段，但其固有的慢速采集特性限制了分析吞吐量，难以获得具有统计代表性的微观结构数据。需要一种方法在保持微观结构分析精度的同时大幅提升EBSD采集效率。

Method: 采用生成对抗网络（SRGAN）构建机器学习超分辨率框架，以NMC正极颗粒的EBSD数据训练模型，对低分辨率数据进行计算增强。将SRGAN与经典插值方法在不同放大倍数（2x至12x）下进行系统对比，通过定性图像指标和定量微观结构分析（如晶粒面积等效直径、最大球内切直径、晶界长度等）评估性能。

Result: SRGAN在各项指标上系统性地优于经典插值方法，尤其在保留小晶粒和维持真实晶界方面。5倍放大因子（对应25倍采集加速或25倍视野扩大）被证明可行，此时晶粒面积等效直径、最大球内切直径和晶界长度的相对误差分别为+5.7%、+8.2%和-14.6%。

Conclusion: SRGAN超分辨率框架显著提升了EBSD采集效率，使获得更具统计稳健性的微观结构数据集成为可能，将EBSD定位为材料研究和工业过程开发中的高通量表征工具。

Abstract: Quantitative microstructural characterization of Li-ion battery electrode materials using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) has been proven as a critical method for optimizing cell performance. However, the inherently slow nature of EBSD can hinder the throughput of analyses needed for statistical representation of a material microstructure being developed. This work demonstrates a machine learning super-resolution framework using a generative adversarial network (SRGAN) to significantly increase EBSD throughput. The SRGAN model was trained on EBSD data of LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC) cathode particles to computationally enhance low-resolution datasets and its performance is compared against classical interpolation methods across various upscaling factors (2x to 12x). Both qualitative image metrics and quantitative microstructural analysis verified that the SRGAN systematically outperformed classical methods, particularly in preserving small grains and maintaining realistic grain boundaries. We demonstrate that a 5x upscaling factor, corresponding to a 25x speed-up in acquisition time or a 25x larger field of view, is practical while maintaining acceptable accuracy in key metrics like grain size and shape. For instance, at 5x upscaling, relative errors were +5.7%, +8.2%, and -14.6% on grain area-equivalent diameter, grain maximum sphere-inscribed diameter, and grain boundary length, respectively. The SRGAN methodology developed in this work significantly enhances the efficiency of EBSD acquisition for more statistically robust microstructural dataset, enabling EBSD as a high-throughput characterization tool for materials research and industrial process development.

</details>


### [304] [Discretizing Continuous Time Series for Imputation with Masked Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19119)
*Dongbin Kim,Seungyun Lee,Geonwoo Shin,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MDTIM（Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model），利用掩码扩散模型的训练范式进行时间序列插补，通过结构正交的MASK token和直接预测原始值来对齐表征与学习目标，并引入随机离散化方法弥合离散掩码扩散与连续时间序列之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法存在两个局限：1）缺失值与观测值嵌入在相同表示空间中，缺乏显式结构分离；2）基于连续扩散的方法被训练为预测添加的噪声而非原始信号，与插补任务的学习目标不一致。

Method: 1) 采用掩码扩散模型的训练范式，将MASK token设计为与有效观测值结构正交；2) 模型直接预测原始值而非噪声；3) 引入Stochastic Discretization（随机离散化）方法，将连续时间序列值映射为具有序数感知的离散token，同时保留连续动态特性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，MDTIM在多种缺失场景下展现出优越的鲁棒性和可扩展性，持续优于最新的确定性和生成式基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过在表示空间和学习目标两个层面同时对齐插补任务，MDTIM有效解决了现有方法的局限性，验证了掩码扩散范式在连续时间序列插补上的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Time series imputation is a crucial area for reliable time series analysis, yet it remains challenging due to the complex temporal dynamics and noise of real-world data. Existing approaches, however, exhibit two limitations: missing and observed values are embedded within the same representation space without explicit structural separation, and continuous diffusion-based methods are trained to predict added noise rather than the original signal. To address these, we propose the Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM), which leverages the training paradigm of masked diffusion model for imputation tasks. The MASK token is structurally orthogonal to valid observations, and the model directly predicts the original values, naturally aligning both the representation and the learning objective with the imputation task. To bridge the gap between discrete masked diffusion and the continuous, ordinal nature of time series, we further introduce Stochastic Discretization, which maps continuous values to ordinal-aware tokens while preserving continuous dynamics. Our experiments on diverse benchmarks confirm that MDTIM achieves superior robustness and scalability, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines across various missing scenarios.

</details>


### [305] [PGFS++: Molecular Property Improvement under Synthesis and Diversity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19121)
*Boqiao Zhang,Godbless James,Sai Krishna Gottipati,Andrew Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGFS++，一个合成感知的强化学习框架，用于输入特异性的分子改进。它通过学习反应模板和库存构建块，在保持输出多样性和结构相似性的同时改进分子目标属性，解决了先前PGFS及PGFS+方法中反应物选择间接和奖励黑客（输出多样性坍塌）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现中优化分子属性（如类药性、结合亲和力）是常见任务，但无约束化学空间中优化的分子若无法合成则实际价值有限。已有的合成感知强化学习方法PGFS通过反应物嵌入预测进行反应物选择，方式间接，学习效果受限。

Method: 首先提出PGFS+，使用可训练的嵌入查找表来表示反应模板和第二反应物，结合更有效的评分函数和RL算法。然后发现PGFS+存在奖励黑客问题——强大的反应物搜索将多样输入映射到同一高奖励分子，导致输出多样性坍塌。为此提出PGFS++：将输入分子作为正向合成轨迹的起点，应用学习到的反应模板与兼容的库存构建块，生成目标属性改进、具有明确合成路线且与输入结构相似的分子。

Result: 实验表明PGFS++在分子改进任务上能够在改进目标属性的同时保持高输出多样性，显著优于PGFS和PGFS+。

Conclusion: PGFS++是一种有效的合成感知分子改进框架，兼顾属性改进、可合成性和输出多样性，为早期药物发现中的分子优化提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Improving molecular properties, such as drug-likeness or binding affinity, is a recurring task in early-stage drug discovery. However, molecules optimized in an unconstrained chemical space have limited practical value if they cannot be synthesized. Policy Gradient for Forward Synthesis (PGFS) is a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning method for molecular improvement, but its use of reactant embedding prediction makes reactant selection indirect, which, as we show, limits learning effectiveness. We first develop PGFS+, in which reaction templates and second reactants are represented by trainable embedding lookup tables. Combined with a more effective scoring function and RL algorithm, PGFS+ significantly improves the desired property. However, it exposes a reward-hacking failure mode: a powerful reactant search can map diverse input molecules to the same high-reward magnet molecule, improving the reward while collapsing the output diversity. We therefore introduce PGFS++, a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning framework for input-specific molecular improvement. Given an input molecule, PGFS++ treats it as the start of a forward-synthesis trajectory, applies learned reaction templates with compatible in-stock building blocks, and produces a molecule with improved target properties, an explicit synthesis route, and structural similarity to the input. Experiments on molecular improvement tasks show that PGFS++ improves target properties while preserving high output diversity.

</details>


### [306] [Leaf Values as Coordinates: Exact Contrastive Explanation for Gradient-Boosted Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19127)
*Emanuele Luzio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将梯度提升集成的叶值重新解读为高维空间中的坐标，使模型在R^M空间中线性作用于样本，从而实现可精确解释的对比分析和可行的反事实建议（recourse recommendation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升树是表格数据中最常用的模型之一，但其决策过程的解释性较差。现有的反事实解释和recourse方法通常依赖采样、近似或对特征可加性的假设，无法精确追溯到模型内部的具体决策路径。作者希望从表示层面找到一个让解释"天然精确"的视角。

Method: 将梯度提升集成中每棵树贡献的叶值视为M维空间中一个坐标，而非中间结果。于是每个实例映射为R^M中的一个点，模型对该点做线性加和得到得分。基于此表示构建反事实解释和recourse方法：两个实例的差异向量在共享叶子处为零，因此只由少数坐标承载，每个坐标可追溯到具体树中的具体分裂。Recourse方法在此表示上直接优化。

Result: 在五个表格数据集上经重复交叉验证评估：recommendation能以6.2e-15的精度重建模型自身决策；在信贷数据集上，其推荐在努力程度与真实感方面Pareto占优；当推荐限制为样本实际可执行的改变（排除年龄、已定 delinquency 等不可变特征）时，本方法保留58%的有效性，而最强基线仅保留41%。

Conclusion: 通过将叶值重新解读为坐标，梯度提升模型的加性结构在正确的空间中自然地显现，从而无需拟合、采样或特征可加性假设即可获得精确的对比解释。该方法在可验证性、Pareto最优性和实际可执行性上均优于基线，且揭示了一个标准评估无法捕捉的关键维度：推荐是否真的可以被用户执行。

Abstract: A gradient-boosted ensemble predicts by summing one leaf value per tree. Read
  those values as coordinates rather than as intermediate results, and every
  instance becomes a point in R^M on which the model acts linearly: the score is
  the sum of the coordinates.
  This small change of view makes contrastive explanation exact. The difference
  between two instances is a vector that is identically zero wherever they share
  a leaf, so the gap between a rejected applicant and an accepted one is carried
  by a handful of coordinates, each traceable to a real split in a real tree.
  Nothing is fitted, sampled, or assumed additive in features -- the additivity
  is already there, in the right space.
  We build a recourse method on this representation and evaluate it on five
  tabular datasets under repeated cross-validation. Its recommendation
  reconstructs the model's own decision to 6.2 x 10^-15, so an auditor can
  re-check the arithmetic without the model. On the credit datasets it is
  Pareto-non-dominated on effort against realism. And when recommendations are
  restricted to changes the subject could actually make -- not their age, not a
  settled delinquency -- it retains 58% of its validity where the strongest
  baseline retains 41%, a distinction the standard evaluation cannot see because
  it never asks whether a recommendation can be carried out.

</details>


### [307] [Beyond Trial Averaging: Anchoring Neural and Visual Representations for Few-Repetition Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19128)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEAR（神经锚点检索）框架，解决脑信号到图像检索在低重复次数（1-4次）下准确率骤降的问题。NEAR利用高重复次数的中心作为锚点，从查询端和图像端双向逼近锚点，在EEG、MEG和fMRI四个数据集上均显著提升少重复场景下的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑-图像检索方法通常需要平均多达80次神经试验来获取一个图像的高质量表征，导致获取时间长、成本高、用户负担重。在只有一次或少数几次重复时检索准确率大幅下降。作者发现仅将性能下降归因于查询噪声是不充分的——存在非传递性对齐模式：低重复查询信号和高重复中心均与图像表征对齐，但两者之间并不直接对齐，说明检索性能下降不仅是查询噪声问题，候选库（gallery）的放置也影响检索效果。

Method: NEAR框架的核心思想是将高重复次数中心视为锚点，从两侧向锚点逼近：(1) 去噪器将带噪的低重复查询信号拉向真实锚点；(2) 一个小型网络从图像预测其对应的伪锚点，并将图像表征拉向该伪锚点。通过锚定神经表征与视觉表征，使两者在低重复场景下仍能有效对齐。

Result: 在覆盖EEG、MEG和fMRI的四个数据集上，NEAR在少重复（few-repetition）场景下持续提升检索性能。在THINGS-EEG2数据集上，当平均1次和4次重复时，200-way Top-1准确率分别提升了5.7和9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: NEAR通过锚定神经表征与视觉表征，降低了检索对重复采集的依赖，使脑-图像检索更接近实际部署应用。该工作不仅改进了检索性能，还揭示了对"低重复性能下降仅由查询噪声导致"这一常见假设的修正，指出候选库分布同样影响检索效果。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from brain signals probes neural representations and enables neuro-rehabilitation and dream decoding. Recent brain-to-image retrieval approaches have achieved promising performance, typically by averaging many (up to 80) neural trials per image, requiring repeated stimulus presentation that increases latency, cost, and user burden. When only one or a few repetitions are available, the retrieval accuracy drops sharply. This drop is commonly attributed to query noise because averaging suppresses noise and increases signal stability. However, we find a non-transitive alignment pattern: the low-repetition query signal and the image representation each align with the high-repetition center, but not directly with each other. This pattern shows that query noise is only part of the problem and that gallery placement also affects retrieval. We therefore propose a neural-anchor-based retrieval (NEAR) framework that treats the high-repetition center as an anchor and approaches it from both sides: a denoiser pulls the noisy query toward the true anchor, and a small network predicts each candidate's pseudo anchor from its image and pulls the image toward it. Across four datasets spanning EEG, MEG and fMRI, NEAR consistently improved retrieval in the few-repetition regime. On THINGS-EEG2, it improved 200-way Top-1 accuracy by 5.7 and 9.3 percentage points respectively, when averaging one and four repetitions. By anchoring neural and visual representations, NEAR reduces reliance on repeated acquisition and brings neural retrieval closer to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [308] [SCORE: Subject Coordinate Recovery for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19134)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Siyang Li,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCORE框架，通过恢复源subject EEG坐标与部署时目标坐标对齐，实现无标签校准的跨被试EEG-to-image检索，显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-to-image检索方法在新用户（无标注校准数据）上的性能远低于有标注的组内方法，限制了实际部署。作者通过跨被试EEG特征分析发现，不同被试对概念的语义关系保持相似，但表达这些关系所用的坐标方向不同，这为无标签坐标对齐提供了理论基础。

Method: SCORE框架包含两个阶段：(1) 训练阶段使用恢复感知（recovery-aware）源训练，将源被试的EEG与公共图像空间对齐，并通过仅源（source-only）episode模拟不可见被试的坐标恢复过程；(2) 部署阶段在双编码器冻结的情况下，通过hubness校正的匹配选择可靠的EEG-图像锚点，估计正交变换以恢复目标EEG坐标，无需源数据或目标标签。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG2和Alljoined-1.6M两个公开基准的200-way检索任务中，SCORE对所有目标被试均优于未适应基线，取得最佳平均精度，Top-1/Top-5分别为53.23%/83.55%和12.01%/32.16%，超过最强基线17.45/15.70和3.08/4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: SCORE在无需目标标签或编码器更新的条件下，实现了跨用户的EEG图像解码，将基于脑电的视觉解码推向更稳健、实用、低延迟的实际部署。

Abstract: Accurate visual decoding can reveal how the brain represents visual information and recover perceived content from neural signals such as electroencephalography (EEG), with potential for neural communication. However, current EEG-to-image retrieval methods perform far below their within-subject counterparts for new users without labeled calibration, limiting real-world deployment. To understand this gap, we analyze EEG features across subjects and find that different subjects preserve similar relationships among concepts but express them along different coordinate directions. We therefore propose Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE), a target label-free framework combining recovery-aware source training with coordinate alignment at deployment. During training, SCORE aligns source subject EEG with a common image space and simulates unseen-subject recovery through source-only episodes. At deployment, with both encoders frozen, SCORE selects reliable EEG-image landmarks through hubness-corrected matching and estimates an orthogonal transformation to recover target EEG coordinates without source data or target labels. In 200-way retrieval on two public benchmarks, SCORE outperforms the unadapted baseline for every target subject and achieves the best overall accuracy. It reaches 53.23%/83.55% and 12.01%/32.16% Top-1/Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 and Alljoined-1.6M, respectively, surpassing the strongest baselines by 17.45/15.70 and 3.08/4.62 percentage points. Without target labels or encoder updates, SCORE brings brain-based visual decoding closer to robust, practical, low-latency deployment across users.

</details>


### [309] [Bridge Graphical Models: Coupling, Projection, and Current-Preserving Dynamics for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19144)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了连续时间生成模型（如扩散模型、流匹配、Schrödinger桥等）中普遍存在的"桥到解码器压缩"结构瓶颈，提出"马尔可夫化间隙"（Markovization gap）作为训练前可诊断的不可约损失度量，并引入桥图模型（BGM）框架统一比较不同模型族的端到端设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型通常由端点条件的桥（bridge）构建，但生成过程需要一个仅观测当前状态和时间的非预测性马尔可夫解码器。这种从桥到解码器的压缩过程是所有主流生成模型（扩散模型、流匹配、修正流、Schrödinger桥、基于场的生成模型）共同面临的结构性瓶颈。现有工作缺乏对这种固有信息损失的度量与比较框架，导致设计选择难以在训练前做出有依据的判断。

Method: 作者定义了马尔可夫化间隙（Markovization gap），即桥速度在给定马尔可夫状态下的时间积分条件方差，它衡量从端点条件运动预测到采样器可用信息的MMSE（最小均方误差），是一种在任何神经网络训练前就不可约的信息损失。为使该瓶颈在不同模型族间可比较，作者提出桥图模型（BGM）形式体系，将端点耦合、桥定律、马尔可夫投影和保流动力学表示分离为独立设计维度。该形式体系同样可将Poisson和静电模型表示为场线桥核，并定义相应的场线马尔可夫化间隙。在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上的合成、潜在空间和像素空间实验中，用几分钟可估计的特征空间代理间隙（feature-space proxy gap）在训练前即可按与下游训练损失和FID一致的方向对设计选择进行排序。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上，特征空间代理间隙在训练前几分钟内即可完成估计，且对设计选择的排序方向与固定架构、桥、采样器和计算预算下的下游训练损失及FID一致。这验证了马尔可夫化间隙作为训练前诊断指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫化间隙可作为桥和耦合设计的预训练诊断指标，为桥图模型框架下的设计选择提供训练前的信息依据，有助于理解并优化连续时间生成模型的结构性瓶颈。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models are often built from endpoint-conditioned bridges, but generation requires a different object: a non-anticipative Markov decoder that only observes the current state and time. We identify this bridge-to-decoder compression as a structural bottleneck shared by diffusion models, flow matching, rectified flow, Schrödinger bridges, and field-based generative models. We introduce the \emph{Markovization gap}, the time-integrated conditional variance of the bridge velocity given the Markov state. It is the MMSE of predicting endpoint-conditioned motion from the information available to a sampler, and it measures an irreducible loss incurred before any neural network is trained. To make this bottleneck comparable across model families, we define \emph{Bridge Graphical Models} (BGMs), which separate endpoint coupling, bridge law, Markovian projection, and current-preserving dynamics representation as independent design choices. The same formalism also represents Poisson and electrostatic models as field-line bridge kernels with a corresponding field-line Markovization gap. Across synthetic, latent, and pixel-space pilots on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, a feature-space proxy gap estimated in minutes before training ranks design choices in the same direction as downstream training loss and FID under fixed architecture, bridge, sampler, and compute. These results support the Markovization gap as a pre-training diagnostic for bridge and coupling design.

</details>


### [310] [Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning for Controlled Hawkes Jump-Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19151)
*Tomasz R. Bielecki,Thibaut Mastrolia,Haoze Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非马尔可夫框架下由多元Hawkes过程驱动的随机微分方程（SDE）的随机控制问题，提出了一种有限维马尔可夫化逼近方法和连续时间确定性策略梯度学习算法（Hawkes-CT DDPG），实现了模型无关的非马尔可夫Hawkes驱动优化求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Hawkes过程强度的路径依赖记忆性，该类问题在非马尔可夫核下超出了经典随机控制理论的适用范围。现有方法局限于特定马尔可夫核（如指数核），亟需一种能够处理一般Hawkes核的通用求解框架。

Method: 首先，利用混合指数核逼近多元Hawkes过程，发展有限维马尔可夫化程序并证明其收敛性（包括Hawkes过程本身、强度以及对偶价值函数的收敛）。其次，基于马尔可夫近似问题，提出连续时间确定性策略梯度方法（Hawkes-CT DDPG），该算法仅需观测事件时间、SDE解的实现和选定的衰减滤波器，无需知晓Hawkes核系数，属于模型无关算法。

Result: 在简单指数核、Erlang核和幂律核三种不同类型的核下，将Hawkes-CT DDPG与离散时间强化学习方法进行了系统对比，验证了所提方法的有效性和优势。

Conclusion: 该研究为处理非马尔可夫Hawkes驱动的随机控制问题提供了一条理论保证完备且实用的路径，所提出的马尔可夫化逼近和模型无关的连续时间强化学习算法能够有效扩展到一般核函数场景。

Abstract: We study stochastic control of multivariate Hawkes-driven stochastic differential equations with machine learning algorithms in a non-Markovian setting. Due to the path dependence of the memory of the Hawkes intensity, this problem does not fall within classical stochastic control theory outside particular Markovian kernels. We first develop a finite-dimensional Markovianization procedure and algorithm to approximate multivariate Hawkes processes with mixtures of exponential kernels. We prove the convergence of the Markovianized approximation of the Hawkes process, its intensity, and the value of the problem to the original non-Markovian processes and the value of the primal problem. We then formulate continuous-time deterministic policy gradient learning on the Markovianized approximation of the problem, called Hawkes-CT DDPG. We propose a model-free algorithm to solve the non-Markovian Hawkes-driven optimization by observing only the event times of the process, the realization of the solution to the SDE, and a chosen set of decay filters, while the Hawkes kernel coefficients remain unknown. We compare our continuous time reinforcement learning Hawkes-CT DDPG method with discrete time reinforcement learning techniques under three different types of kernels: simple exponential, Erlang, and power-law kernels.

</details>


### [311] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在124M参数GPT-2模型上注入194-token文本段（共24次反事实实验），测量单个训练样本对最终模型的直接影响。发现注入文本在50步后仍可被模型预测（8/8种子，p<10⁻⁴），但最终权重衰减后差异消失；权重位移达种子间距离的44.1%，而插值损失屏障仅为3.0%，说明样本仅将模型在其吸引盆内重新定位，未移出该盆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献通常只能估计而无法直接测量，因为测量需要对完整预训练运行两次昂贵实验（仅差一行批次）。作者希望真正测量（而非估计）单个样本的因果影响，从而理解训练样本如何在优化动力学中留下痕迹。

Method: 训练32个124M参数的GPT-2模型（4条件×8随机种子），在OpenWebText上从零开始。在训练步200（峰值学习率）时，将256行批次中的一行替换为包含194-token文本的固定上下文注入。四种条件：流畅散文（语料库证实的主题）、流畅散文（虚构主题，梯度delta匹配0.14%以内）、随机键盘字符、无注入对照组。训练结束后在最终权重上测量交叉熵、插值损失屏障、每层中心核对齐（CKA）和权重位移。

Result: 注入50步后，见注入文本的模型预测该文本优于未注入模型0.039和0.044纳特交叉熵（8/8种子，p<10⁻⁴）。但到最终步（9536步），该差异对两段文本分别以p=0.25和p=0.71不可检测（最小可检测效应0.025和0.079纳特）。两文本间差异亦不可检测（p=0.54）。预注册的插值损失屏障对比为+0.0068（p=0.509），最小可检测效应0.032屏障单位；保留交叉熵为-0.00044（p=0.310）。CKA在任何层、任何条件下均无可检测分离。权重位移达到种子间欧氏距离的44.1%，且在训练中点已92%定型；屏障仅达种子间屏障的3.0%，两者相差约15倍（且为下界）。

Conclusion: 单个样本的影响在短时间窗口内可测，但训练后期所有几何度量不可分离。注入将模型在其吸引盆内重新定位（权重位移大），但不改变盆间屏障（屏障小），说明单样本不能将模型移出其泛化盆地。样本贡献在复杂损失景观中衰减，最终几何/泛化结果对单个训练例不敏感。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [312] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出组校准在线蒸馏（GC-OPD），用于长上下文任务。作者发现在线蒸馏（OPD）中教师提供的token级指导与任务级验证器奖励在长输入下存在分歧，因此引入组级相对残差校准，将验证器奖励与OPD分数的组内归一化差值作为带符号分歧残差，并通过相对优势信用分配（RACA）将其分配到token上。在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD将Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的平均分从29.08/35.12提升至40.47/44.65，优于vanilla OPD（39.31/43.56）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文任务中，在线蒸馏（OPD）依赖教师提供的token级密集指导，但这类指导倾向于生成局部看似合理却遗漏跨输入分布的证据或违反全局约束的响应。与此相对，任务级验证器在响应层面评估任务完成度并返回反映部分成功的分级奖励。教师与验证器之间的这种分歧在长输入场景下愈发显著，成为蒸馏效果的瓶颈。

Method: GC-OPD包含两个核心步骤：(1) 在每个rollout组内，分别对验证器奖励和轨迹级OPD分数进行归一化，计算二者的差值作为带符号的教师-验证器分歧残差；(2) 利用相对优势信用分配（RACA），根据token的相对OPD优势将该轨迹级残差分配给各token，同时保留原始OPD信号。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准上，GC-OPD将官方Qwen3-4B的平均分从29.08提升至40.47，将Qwen3-8B从35.12提升至44.65；对照组vanilla OPD分别仅达39.31和43.56。消融实验表明带符号残差优于额外的OPD派生项或直接组归一化验证器奖励加法，RACA优于均匀token分配。

Conclusion: 组相对残差校准能够在不丢弃密集token级指导的前提下有效引入验证器结果，弥补教师与验证器之间的不一致，显著提升长上下文任务的蒸馏性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>


### [313] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lévy Attention——一种跨注意力算子，利用不均匀泊松随机测度将softmax注意力替换为随机积分。它能以闭式形式、零额外成本地从确定性前向传播中同时给出预测及其不确定性估计（证据质量与值域离散度），在不损失精度的情况下为不规则采样时间序列模型提供置信度报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型在不规则采样时间序列上可以在任意连续时间戳回答查询，但无法说明每个答案的可信度。现有方法要么额外训练不确定性头、要么依赖多次MC采样，代价高昂。作者希望利用注意力层本身的结构，让产生预测的同一次前向传播也能闭式地报告预测的不确定性。

Method: 将softmax交叉注意力替换为Lévy Attention：查询-键兼容性在连续（时间×通道）索引空间上构造一个不均匀泊松随机测度的强度函数；测度在该强度下散布原子；输出对这些原子处插值后的值场取平均。期望上等价于一个软化的余弦核注意力，因此可替代softmax层并用精确梯度训练。泊松构造以闭式形式保留了softmax丢弃的信息：证据Λ_q（总兼容性质量）和值离散度trΣ_V(q)，并通过精确方差恒等式组合为校准的不确定性估计σ̂(q)。

Result: 在t-PatchGNN上，算子替换相比匹配对照组最多损失5.6%准确率，在最高稀疏数据集上无损。不确定性信号（离散度）在20次MC dropout之上有改善（跨五组种子比较）。校准后的高斯分布其零样本CRPS优于五十次采样器；split-conformal包装器在各水平上达到名义覆盖率；单次前向在1.4秒内对3,383名未见患者按信任度排序。

Conclusion: Lévy Attention证明注意力层自身就能以零额外成本输出预测及不确定性，且稀疏数据下证据因子提供强信息，密集数据下离散度才是关键信号。该方法在几乎不损失精度的情况下提供校准可靠的置信度估计，为不规则采样时间序列推理带来实用且高效的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [Scheduling and Routing with Degradation-Triggered Job Arrivals: An Application to Forest Firefighting with an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18140)
*Erdi Dasdemir,Esther Jose,Rajan Batta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交织的调度与路由问题，其中新任务因现有任务的退化而出现，并以航空森林消防为背景，构建了最大化价值保留的混合整数规划模型和基于动态约束生成的混合模型，通过计算实验和案例研究验证了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题背后体现了"一针及时省九针"的谚语理念：推迟干预会导致现有任务（如火灾）恶化并扩散，进而触发新任务的出现。在航空森林消防场景中，这一问题的实际应用意义显著，需要有效的调度与路由策略来最大化区域价值保留。

Method: 首先定义交织调度与路由问题的数学表达，其中每个作业位置具有初始默认奖励，未处理作业会逐渐降低该值，且需求超过阈值时会触发新作业到达。然后开发最大化价值保留的混合整数规划（MIP）模型，并进一步提出基于动态约束生成的混合模型以提升可扩展性。

Result: 通过计算实验和案例分析评估了模型的性能与实际可行性，验证了所提方法在森林消防场景中的有效性，并公开了代码库以保证研究的可复现性。

Conclusion: 该研究为森林火灾背景下的调度与路由决策提供了一种集成建模方法，兼顾了任务退化动态与奖励保留目标，并通过动态约束生成技术增强了模型的实用规模。

Abstract: We define an intertwined scheduling and routing problem where new jobs appear due to the degradation of the existing jobs. Specifically, once a job arrives at a potential job location, a time window begins during which the demand of the job can be fulfilled. The demand degrades within the time window, and once it surpasses a particular threshold, it triggers the arrival of new jobs. Each job location inherently possesses an initial default reward, and the presence of an unprocessed job at a location gradually reduces this default value. The overall objective is to maximize the total remaining reward. The underlying motivation of this problem aligns with the proverb ``a stitch in time saves nine," and the problem itself carries practical implications. We focus on the problem in the context of aerial forest firefighting. Each ignited area has a designated action window; delaying intervention causes the fire to grow, diminishing the area's value and causing it to spread to adjacent areas. We develop a mixed-integer programming model that maximizes value retention in wildfire-threatened regions, and a hybrid model based on dynamic constraint generation to enhance the scalability of the model. We evaluate the performance and practicality of our models through computational experiments and a case study. Additionally, we ensure the study's reproducibility and encourage further research by providing open access to the codebase of our model.

</details>


### [315] [Trust as a Field: A Macroscopic Representation for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18178)
*Md Mahmudul Islam,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合为道路段上的连续表示，并研究了从稀疏路侧单元测量中重建完整信任场的问题，提出了一种场信息深度学习方法的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信任评估方法主要作用于单车层面，难以推理信任在道路段上的时空演化规律，缺乏跨路段、跨时间的连续信任表示能力。

Method: 提出时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合为道路段上的连续场表示。信任场在道路段上形式化定义。基于合成轨迹做可控仿真实验。比较两种重建方法：基于坐标的深度学习基线（从稀疏样本学习通用信任场）和场信息深度学习方法（将信任视为车辆携带的潜在量，通过聚合机制强制测量一致性）。

Result: 场信息方法能更准确地恢复轨迹对齐的低信任模式，重建误差更低。

Conclusion: 该框架为协作式互联车辆系统中的信任评估提供了时空连续视角，且场信息深度学习在稀疏RSU测量下重建信任场具有明显优势。

Abstract: Trust assessment is a fundamental component of cooperative and connected vehicle systems. However, existing approaches operate primarily at the level of individual vehicles, making it difficult to reason about trust evolution across road segments. In this paper, we propose a spatio-temporal trust-field framework that aggregates microscopic vehicle-level trust into a continuous representation over space and time. The trust field is formally defined on road segments. We conducted simulation-based experiments using synthetic trajectories generated under controlled conditions, enabling analysis of trust-field behavior in simple road scenarios. Beyond theoretical modeling, we study an implication of the trust-field concept: reconstructing the full trust field from sparse roadside-unit (RSU) measurements. We compare (i) a coordinate-based deep learning baseline that learns a generic trust field from sparse samples and (ii) a field-informed deep learning method that treats trust as a latent quantity carried by vehicles and enforces measurement consistency through the aggregation mechanism. The field-informed approach more accurately recovers trajectory-aligned low-trust patterns and yields improved reconstruction error.

</details>


### [316] [Revisiting the "Push-T" Robot Manipulation Task with Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18227)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Push-T基准任务，证明LLM编码智能体（Claude Code with Fable 5）无需任何人类演示数据即可通过自主编码循环解决该任务，达到100%成功率且比最优扩散策略少用46%的步数，并扩展到完整字母表（Push-A到Push-Z）及多机器人跨本体仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证新兴Agentic Robotics范式下，LLM编码智能体能否在无需演示数据的情况下，仅通过自主编码与仿真实验解决经典的Push-T操作任务，并探索其在任务扩展性和跨本体泛化方面的潜力。

Method: 使用Claude Code with Fable 5作为LLM编码智能体，通过迭代编码循环：在线搜索发现2D gym仿真环境，利用仿真实验学习推物动力学，反复优化代码策略直至达到100%成功率；随后利用自生成课程将任务从T扩展到完整字母表，并生成Franka和UR5机械臂的3D跨本体仿真代码。

Result: 智能体达到100%成功率，且使用的步数比使用200个人类演示训练的最优扩散策略少46%；成功解决从Push-T扩展到Push-A到Push-Z的全部字母任务；生成Franka与UR5机械臂的3D跨本体可视反馈仿真解决方案。

Conclusion: LLM编码智能体在无需演示数据的情况下即可超越基于人类演示的模仿学习策略，展示了Agentic Robotics在解决和泛化操作任务方面的巨大潜力，重新审视了演示数据在策略学习中的必要性。

Abstract: Push-T is an iconic benchmark for learning manipulation policies from human demonstrations. The robot must use a single point of contact to push a T-shaped block into a target pose. In this short paper, we revisit the Push-T task in the context of emerging advances in Agentic Robotics where an LLM coding agent -- Claude Code with Fable 5 -- is prompted to create an algorithmic solution that does not require any demonstration data. We study how effective the agentic coding loop can solve the Push-T task, and compare the resulting code as policy with the visuomotor imitation learning policy. Results suggest that the agent found the 2D gym simulation online, and used sim experiments to learn push mechanics, iteratively optimizing to achieve 100% success rate using 46% fewer steps than the best diffusion policy trained with 200 human demonstrations. The coding agent also solve extensions from T to the full alphabet (Push-A to Push-Z) using a self generated curriculum and generated simulation code for the Franka and UR5 robot arms in 3D cross-embodiment simulations with visual feedback. Videos, policies and details will be posted online.

</details>


### [317] [GigaBrain-WBC-0.5: A Behavior World Model for Robust Whole-Body Control with Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18234)
*Ziyang Cheng,Tianshu Tang,Jinxin Lan,Xinze Chen,Yuhan Gong,Zhichao Liu,Changzhong Wu,Yahao Mao,Zongyan Deng,Mingxuan Ma,Huasen Xi,Yilong Liu,Yutong Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yang Wang,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaojie Jin,Zheng Zhu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5是首个面向人形全身控制的Behavior World Model（BWM）。它不是纯粹的反应式追踪器，而是训练一个因果Transformer联合预测下一步动作、下一状态及下一潜在行为指令的分布，使执行动作的网络同时建模环境如何塑造其后续行为。通过自动地形标注管线从重定位动作中恢复完整3D接触几何，实现大规模地形标注。部署时复用预测分布来在线检测不合理指令并将其回收到已学习行为上，实现"尽力而为"的任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身动作追踪策略仅能在平地上工作，因为它们在空场景中训练，从未学习地形和物体接触如何重塑自身动力学。它们试图通过不断扩充参考动作库来让策略学会在任何指令下保持平衡，但当可行行为变得依赖于环境时这种方法就失效了。

Method: 1) 训练一个因果Transformer同时预测下一步动作、下一步状态和下一潜在行为指令的分布；2) 自动地形标注管线从重定位动作中恢复完整3D接触几何，使地形标注达到现有动作数据集的规模；3) 部署时利用预测分布在线检测不合理指令并将其回收到已学习行为上。

Result: 在四个测试场景中均取得最高成功率：地形交互81.3%（是最强基线的4.3倍）、不合理指令下83.1%、摔倒恢复99.3%（是最强基线的16.8倍）。硬件实验中在缺失支撑和扰动下表现鲁棒，Unitree G1检查点可经简单微调迁移到Maker L01机器人。

Conclusion: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5证明将世界模型思想引入人体全身控制是有效路径：让执行网络同时预测环境如何影响后续行为，配合大规模自动地形标注和在线指令合理性检测，能够构建一个统一策略，在实时指令、环境交互、不合理指令、摔倒和扰动等多种条件下保持鲁棒。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking policies turn a humanoid into a robust control interface: the teleoperator---or an upstream model---only supplies a coarse movement intent, while the low-level policy keeps the robot balanced and physically feasible. Existing trackers deliver this interface only on flat ground: trained in empty scenes, they never learn how contact with terrain and objects reshapes their dynamics, and they attempt to teach the policy to balance under any command by continually enlarging the reference-motion corpus, which stops working once feasible behaviors become environment-dependent. We present GigaBrain-WBC-0.5, the first Behavior World Model (BWM) for humanoid whole-body control. Rather than a purely reactive tracker, we train a causal Transformer to jointly predict its next action, next state, and the distribution over its next latent behavior command, so the network that acts also models how the environment shapes what it can do next. An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline recovers full 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion, enabling terrain annotation at the scale of existing motion datasets. The predicted distribution is reused at deployment to detect implausible commands online and retract them onto learned behaviors, so the robot attempts tasks in a "best-effort" manner. The result is a unified policy that takes real-time command, interacts with environment, and stays robust to implausible commands, falls, and disturbances. GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves the highest success rate across all four regimes among three large-scale tracker baselines: 81.3% on terrain interaction (4.3x the strongest baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, and 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x the strongest baseline). Hardware trials show robust interaction under missing supports and disturbances; the Unitree G1 checkpoint transfers to the Maker L01 robot with simple fine-tuning.

</details>


### [318] [GAPL: Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning for LLM-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18254)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Zhangkai Wu,Yushuai Li,Tianyi Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GAPL框架，将LLM决策能力与仿真环境动态建模结合，通过闭环系统实现自动驾驶轨迹规划，在四个Highway-env场景中显著降低碰撞率和位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动驾驶轨迹规划中具备语义丰富的规划能力，但存在幻觉推理、对环境动态建模不足以及数值控制精度有限等缺陷，需要一种方法将LLM的高级推理与精确的低层控制有效结合。

Method: GAPL由三个模块组成：LLM驱动的效果评估器（Effect Evaluator）用于结构化多维度动作-效果估计；基于仿真的效果接地器（Effect Grounder）通过模拟器rollout预测动力学一致的效果；效果感知决策器（Effect-Aware Decision Maker）通过蒸馏器将LLM效果估计与仿真结果对齐，并引导PPO策略学习，形成闭环系统。

Result: 在四个Highway-env场景中，GAPL一致优于基线方法，碰撞率平均降低0.76，平均位移误差（ADE）降低0.86，最终位移误差（FDE）降低2.00，平均奖励增益达1.44。

Conclusion: GAPL通过将LLM的语义推理能力与仿真环境动力学接地相结合，有效解决了LLM在自动驾驶规划中的幻觉和精度问题，验证了LLM与强化学习策略优化融合的有效性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving requires both high-level reasoning and precise low-level control. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic-rich planning capabilities, however, their application is limited by hallucinated reasoning, poor grounding in environment dynamics, and limited numerical precision in control. We propose GAPL (Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning), a unified framework that integrates LLM-based effect estimation, simulation-based effect grounding, and policy optimization into a closed-loop system. GAPL consists of three modules: (1) an LLM-based Effect Evaluator for structured multi-dimensional action-effect estimation; (2) a Simulation-based Effect Grounder that predicts dynamics-consistent effects from simulator rollouts; and (3) an Effect-Aware Decision Maker that grounds LLM effect estimates against simulation via a distiller to guide Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based policy learning. Experiments on four Highway-env scenarios demonstrate that GAPL consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average reductions of {0.76, 0.86, 2.00} in collision rate, average displacement error (ADE), and final displacement error (FDE), and an average reward gain of 1.44.

</details>


### [319] [VERAGMIL: Virtual Environment for Scooping Granular Foods with Imitation Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18258)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Diego Dall'Alba,Przemyslaw Korzeniowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VERAGMIL框架，结合高保真模拟器与直观的虚拟现实(VR)界面来录制人类演示数据，用于训练机器人辅助喂食(RAF)系统处理颗粒状食物（如米、豆类）的抓取与搬运任务。实验结果表明，VR演示显著优于3D空间鼠标数据，其中BCQ模型在减少洒落和接近人类性能方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助喂食系统对帮助残疾或运动障碍人士至关重要，但操作颗粒状食物（如米饭、豆类）因其动态物理特性而极具挑战。从人类演示中学习虽有前景，但获取高质量的演示数据十分困难，需要一种既能提供高保真训练环境又能简化演示收集流程的解决方案。

Method: 提出VERAGMIL框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 高保真模拟器，能真实还原颗粒状食物材料、机器人及传感器，支持多种物理特性的食物；2) 直观的VR界面，用于录制演示数据并兼容不同的模仿学习方法。作者使用VR界面和3D空间鼠标两种方式收集演示，训练了BC、BC-RNN和BCQ三种模仿学习模型，并与人类专家基线进行对比。

Result: 在颗粒状食物抓取和搬运任务上，VR界面采集的演示数据显著优于3D空间鼠标数据。在三种模型中，BCQ取得了最佳综合表现，尤其在减少食物洒落方面效果突出，性能接近人类专家水平。所有模型均在成功率、洒落量、对未见食物的泛化能力和任务完成时间四个维度上进行了评估。

Conclusion: VERAGMIL框架被证实能有效训练机器人辅助喂食系统处理颗粒状材料的操作任务，VR演示方式为模仿学习提供了更高质量的数据来源，BCQ模型在颗粒物处理的洒落控制方面展现出接近人类的优秀性能。框架代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Feeding (RAF) systems are essential for assisting individuals with disabilities or motor impairments in eating tasks. Manipulating granular food items, such as rice and beans, poses significant challenges due to their dynamic physical properties. Learning from human demonstrations offers a promising solution, but acquiring high-quality demonstrations is complex. To address this, we present VERAGMIL, a framework that combines a high-fidelity simulator with an intuitive Virtual Reality (VR) interface for recording demonstrations and supporting different imitation learning methods. VERAGMIL provides a realistic environment for training RAF systems to handle granular materials, including robots, sensors, and various food items with distinct physical characteristics. We evaluate VERAGMIL by training three imitation learning models, BC, BC-RNN, and BCQ, on granular scooping and transporting tasks using both VR interface and 3D space mouse demonstrations, comparing them with a human-expert baseline. The models are assessed on success rate, spillage, generalization to unseen food items, and task completion time. Results show that VR-based demonstrations significantly outperform 3D space mouse data, with BCQ achieving the best overall performance, particularly in reducing spillage and approaching human performance. These findings underscore the effectiveness of our framework for training RAF systems in granular material handling. The code for our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/AmanuelErgogo/VERAGMIL.git.

</details>


### [320] [Transferable Tool-Tissue Contact Detection from Stereo Depth in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18270)
*Mingyeung Wu,Zhonghao Zhang,Hao Yang,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用深度图像中的工具-组织最小距离信号进行可靠的接触检测，通过全监督两状态隐马尔可夫模型在六个训练会话上拟合，并在四个留出会话上达到宏F1 0.927和AUPRC 0.980，显著优于基于RGB的外观分类器（宏F1仅0.320），验证了距离信号作为可迁移接触检测线索的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术中可靠的工具-组织接触检测对于交互感知控制和下游力估计至关重要。现有方法大多依赖RGB外观学习接触分类器，难以泛化到不同任务和不同组织。需要一种更具可迁移性的接触检测信号。

Method: 利用双目相机生成的深度图像，在工具边界周围定位空间受限的最小距离补丁，并归约为标量信号-log10|d|。将该信号形式化为全监督两状态隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），以六折留一会话（LOSO）集成方式在六个触诊会话上拟合，决策阈值从池化的折外预测中选择。

Result: 在四个留出测试会话上达到宏F1 0.927和AUPRC 0.980。对比实验表明，基于RGB的接触分类器在相同任务相同模型上表现良好（F1 0.965），但在不同任务/不同模型上性能显著下降，宏F1仅0.320，远低于所提方法。

Conclusion: 工具-组织距离是接触检测中强健且可迁移的线索。基于深度图像的距离信号方法比基于RGB外观的方法具有更好的跨任务、跨组织泛化能力，为机器人辅助手术中的接触感知提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Reliable tool--tissue contact detection can support interaction-aware control and downstream force estimation in robot-assisted surgery. Most existing methods learn a contact classifier from RGB appearance, which is hard to generalize. In this work, we use the depth image generated from a stereo pair to give more information about tool--tissue contact. For each depth frame, we localize a spatially supported minimum-distance patch around the tool boundary and reduce it to a single scalar, $-\log_{10}|d|$; this signal rises and falls in step with ground-truth contact. We formalize this observation with a fully supervised two-state hidden Markov model. We fit this model as a six-fold leave-one-session-out (LOSO) ensemble on six palpation sessions against a single silicone cup-like phantom, with the decision threshold selected from the pooled out-of-fold predictions. It is evaluated on four held-out sessions of three categories: 1. same task on same phantom; 2. same task on different phantom; 3. different task on different phantom. This model reaches held-out macro F1 $0.927$ and AUPRC $0.980$. We further compare against a reproduction of an RGB-based contact classifier from prior work. This RGB-based model achieves high performance on the first category (F1 $0.965$), but substantially lower performance on the other two, resulting in macro F1 $0.320$ across all four sessions. These results indicate that the tool--tissue distance is a strong, transferable cue for contact detection in robot-assisted surgery.

</details>


### [321] [GuideFetch: A Task Coordination Framework for Concurrent Navigation and Object Retrieval in Assistive Robot Dogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18292)
*Qian Yin,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jianxiang Man,Isik Baran Sandan,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GuideFetch，一个用于异构导引-取物双机器人团队在并发导航与物体取回任务中的协调框架。LLM根据自然语言指令生成四动作模式，执行前经过别名归一化、目标/能力/调度验证，执行时由机器人及物体状态驱动顺序与并行执行。在90组场景×种子的配对比实验中（共360次执行），所有180次在线LLM响应均无需回退或重放即通过验证且与脚本计划一致；顺序与并行执行分别达到72/90和71/90的操作成功率；在两种调度均完成的56个案例中，并行执行平均缩短41.3%的makespan。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作场景中，语法上有效的LLM计划可能违反实体约束（如机器人能力、目标可用性），且控制器表面上的成功动作并不等同于任务实际完成。现有方法缺乏一个能同时处理RLM计划验证与执行状态校验的协调框架，以支撑异构机器人团队的并发任务执行。

Method: GuideFetch框架包含以下核心步骤：(1) LLM从自然语言指令实例化一个受调度约束的四动作模式（four-action schema）；(2) 执行前对机器人、技能和目标别名进行归一化，并将提议动作与注册目标、机器人能力及选定调度进行验证；(3) 执行阶段由机器人状态和物体状态共同驱动，支持顺序与并行两种执行调度，其中导引机器人负责导航带路，取物机器人并发完成物品取回。

Result: 在匹配的2×2研究中（90种场景×种子组合，共360次执行），180次在线LLM响应全部通过验证且与脚本计划完全一致，无需fallback或replay。顺序执行操作成功72/90次，并行执行71/90次。在56个两种调度均成功的案例中，并行执行将平均makespan降低了41.3%。

Conclusion: 在受控环境中，角色分工（导引vs取物）与动作重叠能显著缩短已完成任务的耗时；同时，通过状态检查可以区分"计划有效性"与"任务完成验证"这两个不同层面的保证。该工作强调了并发执行和实体约束验证在LLM驱动的多机器人协调中的重要性。

Abstract: Consider a robot guide dog escorting a blind user to an available seat while a second assistive robot dog concurrently retrieves a cup of coffee and delivers it to the same seat. This setting motivates concurrent execution because navigation and object retrieval can overlap. A syntactically valid Large Language Model (LLM) plan may still violate embodiment constraints, and successful-looking controller motion does not by itself establish task completion. We introduce \textsc{GuideFetch}, a coordination framework for concurrent navigation and object retrieval by a heterogeneous guider and fetcher team. An LLM instantiates a schedule-conditioned four-action schema from a natural-language instruction. Before execution, robot, skill, and target aliases are normalized, and proposed actions are validated against registered targets, robot capabilities, and the selected schedule. Robot and object states then govern sequential and parallel execution. In a matched $2\times2$ study across 90 combinations of scene and seed (360 executions), all 180 online LLM responses validate without fallback or replay and match the corresponding scripted plans. For each planner source, sequential and parallel execution achieve $72/90$ and $71/90$ operational successes, respectively. Among the 56 cases completed by both schedules, parallel execution reduces mean makespan by 41.3\%. Within this controlled setting, role specialization and action overlap shorten completed missions, while state checks distinguish plan validity from verified mission completion. Source code will be available.

</details>


### [322] [A Task-Agnostic Control Strategy for Dynamic Assistance with Pneumatically Actuated Soft Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18364)
*Anoush Sepehri,Zachary Huang,Raymond de Callafon,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逆模型控制的策略，用于气动软体外骨骼助力，仅依赖运动学传感即可提供任务无关的动态助力，无需生理或力传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动人工肌肉为上肢软体外骨骼提供了新的机会，但其复杂动力学和有限带宽使其难以根据用户意图提供响应式助力，这是一个长期挑战。

Method: 采用Hammerstein动态模型（由Preisach磁滞模型和线性时不变滤波器组成）对人工机器人系统建模，仅用140秒数据实现用户个性化，并近似逆模型集成到控制回路中。

Result: 在模拟腕部软助力外骨骼的测试平台上，控制器将交互扭矩降低最多73%，关键屈肌和伸肌的激活降低最多47%，速度范围8°/s至120°/s。

Conclusion: 该控制策略可在无需生理或力传感器解读用户意图的情况下，为气动软体外骨骼提供任务无关的动态助力。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have provided new opportunities to develop upper-extremity soft exosuits for reha- bilitation, augmentation, and assisted daily living. However, the complex dynamics and limited bandwidth of these actuators has made providing responsive assistance based on user intention a longstanding challenge. In this work, we present an inverse-plant control strategy for pneumatically actuated soft exosuits that only relies on kinematic sensing for task-agnostic and dynamic assistance during daily living. We model the human-robot system using a Hammerstein dynamic model, consisting of a Preisach hysteresis model and a linear time-invariant filter, to capture the static and dynamic behavior of the system. We personalize our model to each user using 140 s of data and approximate an inverse to integrate into our control loop. When evaluated on a test rig that emulated a soft assistive exosuit for the wrist, our controller reduced the interaction torque by up to 73% and the activation of key flexor and extensor muscles by up to 47% relative to the condition with no assistance for speeds ranging from 8°/s to 120°/s. Overall, this work presents a control strategy that can provide task-agnostic, dynamic assistance with pneumatically actuated soft exosuits without the need for physiological or force sensors to interpret user intention.

</details>


### [323] [The Embodiment Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18433)
*Yukiyasu Domae,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Masaki Murooka,Takuma Yagi,Yoshiaki Bando,Ryo Hanai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身差距"（embodiment gap）概念，指可复用模型/表示/数据与在目标机器人上实际执行之间的差距，并通过两轴图谱和报告框架系统梳理跨具身机器人基础模型的可复用性与适配工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（RFM，包括VLA策略）常以扩展视角讨论——更多数据、更大模型、更广基准应提升泛化能力。然而在机器人领域，模型能泛化但仍有工作需要在特定身体（具身）的机器人上完成部署，且不同方法与目标机器人之间的适配工作量差异显著，直接影响实际应用。现有讨论缺少对这一差距的系统性分析。

Method: 1. 提出"具身差距"概念，定义可复用组件与目标机器人执行之间的间距。2. 构建两轴分类图谱：横轴为共享结构的类型，纵轴为在目标机器人上执行所需适配的阶段。3. 通过三个重叠研究方向综述近期工作：共享语义与感知、共享机器人数据与接口、跨具身学习对应关系。4. 提出报告框架，识别跨具身学习比较时应检查的适配工作量，而不只报告成功率。

Result: 论文提供了一个结构化的跨具身机器人学习综述框架，识别出不同方法在共享结构类型和执行适配阶段上的分布，并提出了一个超越成功率单一指标的报告框架，以揭示新机器人上仍需完成的适配工作。

Conclusion: 跨具身泛化不能仅用成功率衡量，真正的部署成本取决于具体方法在目标机器人上仍需付出的适配工作。该报告框架有助于更公平地比较跨具身学习方法，并为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Robot foundation models (RFMs), including vision-language-action (VLA) policies, are often discussed through a scaling view: more data, larger models, and broader benchmarks should improve generalization. In robotics, however, a model can generalize while work still remains before it can run on a robot with a particular body. The work required differs across methods and target robots, and those differences affect practical deployment. We call the gap between reusable models, representations, or data and their use in execution on the target robot the embodiment gap. This survey examines what can be reused across robot embodiments and what must still be implemented on a new robot. We place existing methods on a two-axis map that shows the type of shared structure and the stage at which adaptation is needed for execution on the target robot. We then examine recent work through three overlapping research directions: sharing semantics and perception, sharing robot data and interfaces, and learning correspondence across embodiments. We also propose a reporting framework for adaptation work that success rate alone does not reveal. The framework identifies the work that should be checked when comparing cross-embodiment learning and highlights work that remains on a new robot and questions for future study.

</details>


### [324] [HarvestPoint-ACT: Explicit Target Selection and Harvest-Point Conditioning for Robotic Fruit Harvesting under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18446)
*Hanying Hu,Weipeng Li,Yikun Huang,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarvestPoint-ACT，一种用于机器人水果采摘的端到端模仿学习框架，通过显式的实例分割、关键点预测（收获点）和调度器来解决遮挡导致的果物丢失和夹取点推断困难问题，在冠层模型上达到88%成功率，重度遮挡下为75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习虽然避免了手工设计接近和抓取的运动，但策略仍须自行决定采摘哪个水果以及在何处闭合夹爪。遮挡会使策略在收获过程中丢失已选水果，且仅凭像素难以准确推断正确的闭合点，这两个关键决策缺乏显式处理。

Method: 提出HarvestPoint-ACT框架：(1) 实例分割前端配备关键点分支，为每个可见水果预测掩膜和收获点（夹爪闭合位置）；(2) 调度器根据遮挡程度和旅行距离对候选水果排序并选择目标，每次尝试后因冠层变化而重新检测和重排；(3) 所选水果被编码为八维状态（绝对收获点、夹爪到该点的向量、有效性标志、置信度分数）输入动作分块Transformer；(4) 当所选水果暂时未被检测到时，保留机器人基座坐标系中最后的收获点估计并标记为过期，若丢失持续则中止尝试。

Result: 在冠层模拟装置上，HarvestPoint-ACT达到了88%的成功率，在重度遮挡条件下成功率为75%。

Conclusion: 通过将水果选择和夹取点决策显式化到感知前端，并结合调度重用与过期机制，HarvestPoint-ACT有效缓解了遮挡对采摘策略的影响，验证了显式决策在提升端到端模仿学习采摘鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end imitation learning avoids hand-made robot motion for approaching and grasping, but the policy must still decide which fruit to pick and where to close the gripper. Occlusion can make the policy lose the selected fruit during harvesting, and the correct closing point is difficult to infer from pixels alone. This paper presents HarvestPoint-ACT, which makes both decisions explicit in perception and provides them to the policy. An instance segmentation front end with a keypoint branch predicts a mask and a harvest point for each visible fruit, where the harvest point specifies the location to close the gripper. A scheduler ranks detected candidates by occlusion and travel distance and selects one target. After each attempt, it redetects and reranks the candidates because the canopy may have changed. The selected fruit is encoded for an action chunking transformer as an eight-dimensional state, containing the absolute harvest point, the vector from the gripper to that point, a validity flag, and a confidence score. When the selected fruit is temporarily undetected, the system retains the last harvest point estimate in the robot base frame and marks it as stale, and aborts the attempt if the loss persists. On a canopy mock-up, HarvestPoint-ACT achieves a success rate of 88%, and of 75% under heavy occlusion.

</details>


### [325] [Backward Layout Search for Sequence-Constrained Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18454)
*Xi Zhang,Jiancong Dai,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Backward Layout Search (BLS)，通过逆向装配顺序为零件分配初始位姿，利用逐步几何、运动学、抓取和预定运动检查，配合碰撞掩码和候选集过滤，高效生成无碰撞的可执行机器人装配布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配布局规划需要确定装配位置和每个零件的初始位姿，并确保在预定装配序列中无碰撞执行。由于每一步装配后障碍环境都会变化，且工作空间中未装配的零件可能阻挡机器人运动，导致问题具有高度挑战性。

Method: 观察到每个装配步骤的可行性仅取决于当前零件及后续装配零件的初始位姿。基于此依赖关系，提出BLS：按逆向装配顺序分配初始零件位姿。每次扩展执行几何、运动学、抓取和预定运动检查；通过碰撞掩码和候选集过滤去除不可行的初始位姿候选；利用束搜索保留有前景的部分布局；最终以正向装配顺序通过完整运动规划验证完整布局。

Result: 在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能生成无碰撞的可执行布局，相比匹配的正向搜索方法减少了步骤评估次数和搜索时间。

Conclusion: 逆向搜索策略利用了问题本身的依赖性结构，有效剪枝了搜索空间，相较于正向搜索在效率和可行性上均表现更优。

Abstract: Robotic assembly layout planning must determine the assembly site and the initial pose of each part while ensuring collision-free execution of a prescribed assembly sequence. This problem is challenging because the obstacle environment changes after each assembly step, and unassembled parts re maining in the workspace may block robot motions. We observe that the feasibility of each assembly step depends only on the initial poses of the current and later-assembled parts. Based on this dependency, we propose Backward Layout Search (BLS), which assigns initial part poses in reverse assembly order. Each expansion performs geometric, kinematic, grasp, and prescribed-motion checks, while collision masks and candidate set filtering remove infeasible initial part pose candidates. Promising partial layouts are retained through beam selection, and complete layouts are validated by full motion planning in forward assembly order. Experiments on five assembly models show that BLS produces collision-free executable layouts and reduces step evaluations and search time compared with a matched forward search.

</details>


### [326] [DevGRU: Depth-guided Visual Navigation using a Collision-aware Recurrent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18470)
*Kyung Min Han,Eunsom Kim,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DevGRU，一种基于深度图像和点目标条件的导航系统，通过动作预测器和碰撞预测器生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹，在复杂室内环境（结构化布局和狭窄通道）中显著优于ViNT、NoMaD和NavDP等基线方法，同时模型参数量小、推理速度快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航基础模型虽然旨在跨平台泛化，但在复杂室内环境中（特别是结构化布局和狭窄通道）容易发生碰撞。这限制了它们在实际部署中的可靠性和安全性，因此需要一种能够有效避免即时障碍的导航系统。

Method: DevGRU采用深度图像和点目标作为条件输入，核心包含两个组件：(1) 动作预测器（AP），生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹以躲避即时障碍；(2) 碰撞预测器，与AP配合补偿目标姿态估计中累积的误差，并主动缓解未来偏差。

Result: 在九个不同场景和多个基线（ViNT、NoMaD、NavDP及四个变体）上实验，DevGRU在导航性能上大幅超越ViNT和NoMaD；同时参数量少，推理速度最快，相比NavDP模型尺寸小7倍、推理时间快17倍。

Conclusion: DevGRU证明了在复杂室内环境中，通过动作预测与碰撞预测的协同设计，可以在保持高效推理的同时实现更安全可靠的导航，是现有通用导航基础模型的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation models often aim to develop foundation models that can generalize robot navigation across diverse platforms. However, many of these models are prone to collisions when deployed in complex indoor environments, particularly in structured layouts and narrow passages. To address this problem, we propose a depth image- and point-goal-conditioned navigation system, DevGRU. The proposed system employs an action predictor (AP) that generates collision-aware future trajectories, enabling effective avoidance of immediate obstacles. In conjunction with a collision predictor, the AP further compensates for errors accumulated in the goal pose estimation and proactively mitigates future deviations. To evaluate our method, we conducted experiments across nine different scenes and three state-of-the-art approaches - ViNT, NoMaD, and NavDP - as well as four additional variants of ViNT and NoMaD. In terms of navigation performance, DevGRU significantly outperforms ViNT and NoMaD by a large margin. In addition, the proposed model has a relatively small number of trainable parameters, resulting in the fastest inference time among the baselines, particularly outperforming NavDP by 7x in model size and 17x in inference time.

</details>


### [327] [An Experimental Study of Downwash Effects on a Continuum Manipulator Integrated with a Multirotor UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18507)
*Anuraj Uthayasooriyan,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,ernando Vanegas,Felipe Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了多旋翼平台上肌腱驱动连续体机械臂（CM）在旋翼下洗气流影响下的运动学偏差，量化了自由空间及近壁/近地场景中的末端位姿偏差，并提出了基于恒定曲率（CC）引导的高斯过程回归（GPR）残差模型，使前向位姿预测的RMSE大幅改善（位置降低89-95%，姿态降低47-79%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体臂空中操作系统的柔顺性与灵巧性使其适用于受限或危险环境，但螺旋桨下洗气流（尤其在墙壁和地面附近）会降低系统性能。然而，下洗气流对连续体机械臂的动力学影响此前尚未被表征，缺乏系统性的实验量化与补偿模型。

Method: 在静止空气条件下，将肌腱驱动连续体机械臂与恒定曲率（CC）模型进行对比；随后在四个螺旋桨油门等级下测量自由空间中由下洗引起的末端执行器位姿偏差，并在最大油门时测量近壁和近地场景下的偏差；最后基于实验数据学习一个CC引导的高斯过程回归（GPR）残差模型，用于前向位姿预测。

Result: 垂直位置和偏航角表现出最大偏差，且地面效应会放大这些偏差。CC引导的GPR残差模型显著提升了前向位姿预测精度，位置RMSE改善89-95%，姿态RMSE改善47-79%。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统表征了下洗气流对连续体空中机械臂的影响，提出的下洗感知建模方法支持面向补偿的连续体臂空中操作系统建模，为实际应用中的位姿补偿与控制提供了基础。

Abstract: Continuum arm aerial manipulation systems leverage soft-manipulator compliance and dexterity for tasks in confined or hazardous environments, but propeller downwash can degrade performance, particularly near walls and the ground. This effect remains uncharacterized for continuum manipulators. This letter experimentally studies downwash-induced kinematic deviations of a tendon-driven continuum manipulator integrated with a multirotor platform. Under still-air conditions, the CM is compared with a constant-curvature (CC) model. Downwash- induced end-effector pose deviations are then quantified relative to the mean still-air experimental baseline at four propeller throttle levels in free space, and at maximum throttle near a wall, and near the ground. Vertical position and yaw show the largest deviations and are amplified by ground effect. A CC-guided Gaussian process regression (GPR) residual model is learned from experimental data that improves forward pose prediction RMSE (position by 89-95%, orientation by 47-79%), and support compensation-oriented, downwash-aware modeling of continuum arm aerial manipulation systems.

</details>


### [328] [Real-Time Control-Constrained DDP for Underactuated Balancing of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18552)
*SeongWon Nam,Hyunyong Lee,Hansol Kang,Jiman Park,Yeongwoo Son,Bumsu Yi,Jaeyoung Oh,Hyouk Ryeol Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于欠驱动腿式机器人的实时控制约束差分动态规划（DDP）框架（ABC-DDP），通过加速投影梯度法高效求解控制约束问题，并在统一的MPC框架下实现了静态双腿站立、慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多种动态运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DDP在处理控制约束方面存在局限，无法直接满足欠驱动腿式机器人在实时模型预测控制中的约束需求；现有方法在控制约束处理上需要重复进行KKT矩阵求逆，计算效率低。

Method: 提出基于加速投影梯度（APG）的控制约束DDP（ABC-DDP），在不重复KKT求逆的情况下高效计算约束解并识别活跃集；引入虚拟约束将控制约束整合到可行性驱动的多重打靶框架中，即使从动力学不可行的初始状态出发也能稳定优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在统一的MPC框架下实现了受外部扰动时的静态双腿站立，以及慢速猫步、直立行走和高速奔跑等多样动态运动；这是首次使用实时有限时域MPC实现四足机器人静态双腿站立。

Conclusion: ABC-DDP框架证明了基于加速投影梯度的控制约束处理可以有效提升DDP在欠驱动腿式机器人实时MPC中的适用性，拓宽了DDP类方法在强欠驱动动态运动规划中的应用范围。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time control-constrained Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) framework for underactuated legged robots. To address the limitation of classical DDP in handling control constraints, we propose an Accelerated Projected Gradient (APG)-based control-constrained DDP (ABC-DDP), which efficiently computes constrained solutions and identifies active sets without repeated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) inversions. A virtual constraint is introduced to integrate control constraints within a feasibility-driven multiple-shooting framework, enabling stable optimization even from dynamically infeasible initializations. The proposed method supports real-time model predictive control (MPC) with short horizons under strong underactuation. Simulation results demonstrate static two-leg standing under external disturbances, along with diverse dynamic motions including slow catwalk, upright walking, and high-speed running within a unified MPC framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of static two-leg standing of a quadruped robot achieved using real-time finite-horizon MPC.

</details>


### [329] [LabDex: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Dexterous Manipulation in Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18618)
*Zhipeng Tang,Sihang Chen,Sha Zhang,Peihao Yang,Yan Liu,Wentao Zhao,Xinrui Liu,Rui Huang,Wensheng Du,Yuting Huang,Jiajun Deng,Lidian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabDex是一个面向化学实验室灵巧操作的大规模真实世界数据集与基准测试平台，首次统一了真实世界与仿真平台，通过层次化任务分类（原子技能、组合任务、长时程实验）支持跨级别的机器人学习算法训练与系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室的发展需要机器人具备灵巧操作各种实验器皿和仪器、执行长时程且状态依赖的实验流程的能力，但现有基准测试无法同时涵盖灵巧手的使用、真实实验室交互以及多阶段实验流程，缺乏系统化的训练与评估手段。

Method: LabDex采用层次化任务设计，将化学实验室操作系统地组织为三个相互关联的层级：Atomic Skills（表征基础灵巧操作能力）、Compositional Skills（组合技能）和Long-Horizon Laboratory Workflows（长时程实验流程）。该基准具备跨平台特性，首次在统一框架下整合真实世界和仿真平台，提供标准化的任务定义、示范数据和评估协议。研究中在真实与仿真环境中对代表性机器人学习方法进行了跨层级评估。

Result: 实验结果表明LabDex的任务设计和示范数据具有有效性，基准能够支持现有机器人策略在不同层级的实验室灵巧操作任务上的训练和系统性评估，验证了该基准对教育训练和研究发展的支撑作用。

Conclusion: LabDex为自主实验室机器人的研究与开发提供了基础性支撑，层级化设计不仅能评估端任务性能，还能分析基础灵巧技能如何组合并影响更复杂的实验室操作，填补了该领域的基准空白。

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories hold great promise for accelerating scientific discovery. To achieve this vision, robots are supposed to dexterously manipulate diverse labware and instruments and execute long-horizon, state-dependent experimental procedures. Yet existing benchmarks do not jointly capture dexterous hand use, real-world laboratory interactions, and multi-stage experimental procedures, limiting systematic training and evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce LabDex, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for dexterous manipulation in chemistry laboratories, organized around a hierarchical task taxonomy spanning atomic skills, compositional tasks, and long-horizon experiments. First, LabDex is cross-platform and, for the first time, unifies real-world and simulation platforms under a common framework, providing standardized task definitions, demonstrations, and evaluation protocols. Second, LabDex is large-scale and systematically organizes chemistry laboratory operations into three interconnected levels: Atomic Skills, which characterize fundamental dexterous manipulation capabilities; Compositional Skills; and Long-Horizon Laboratory Workflows. This hierarchical design not only supports the evaluation of end-task performance, but also enables the analysis of how fundamental dexterous skills compose and influence more complex laboratory operations. We conduct cross-level evaluations of representative robot learning methods in both real-world and simulation environments. The experimental results validate the effectiveness of the LabDex task design and demonstration data, and show that the benchmark supports the training and systematic evaluation of existing robotic policies across laboratory dexterous manipulation tasks at different levels, providing a foundation for further research and development of autonomous laboratory robots.

</details>


### [330] [Evaluation of Monocular SLAM Systems on High-Altitude Nadir UAV Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18632)
*Gašper Spagnolo,Matej Dobrevski,Danijel Skočaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地评测了五种单目SLAM系统在航拍俯视视频场景下的性能表现。结果表明，MASt3R-SLAM在五组DJI无人机飞行数据中取得最低水平平均绝对误差（0.53%参考路径长度），DROID-SLAM在整体上表现最优（平均2.88%参考路径长度），但所有系统在长距离大场景轨迹中均存在严重畸变，且垂直方向精度始终较差。结论是当前单目SLAM方法单独使用不足以支撑可靠的纯视觉空中导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍俯视视频同时面临弱几何约束和严重的感知混淆（perceptual aliasing）问题，对单目SLAM构成独特挑战。现有文献缺乏对该特殊场景下主流单目SLAM系统性能的系统性基准测试，因此作者希望在无惯性导航或GNSS辅助的纯视觉条件下，量化各系统的鲁棒性和精度极限，以明确当前技术瓶颈。

Method: 作者选取五种单目SLAM系统，在三个层次的数据集上进行了基准测试：(1) 五组本地无人机（DJI）飞行数据；(2) 合成城市级尺度图像数据；(3) 长距离航拍序列（GES和ALTO数据集）。为隔离视觉性能本身，实验中不提供任何惯性传感器或GNSS辅助信息，以水平平均绝对误差（MAE）占参考路径长度百分比为评价指标。

Result: 性能随环境和轨迹尺度变化显著：MASt3R-SLAM在五组DJI飞行中以0.53%参考路径长度的平均水平MAE表现最佳；在长距离GES和ALTO序列上，没有任何系统能够持续保持全局轨迹形状；整体而言DROID-SLAM表现最优，在完成的运行中平均误差为2.88%参考路径长度。垂直方向精度普遍较差，且即使具备回环闭合能力，大面积轨迹仍存在高度畸变。

Conclusion: 当前单目SLAM方法在航拍俯视场景下的综合性能尚不足以支撑可靠的纯视觉空中导航。弱几何约束与感知混淆的固有困难使得即使性能最好的系统，在大尺度航拍轨迹上也难以维持全局一致性，垂直方向精度更是普遍性短板。

Abstract: Aerial nadir video combines weak geometric constraints with severe perceptual aliasing, making it a difficult regime for monocular SLAM. We benchmark five monocular SLAM systems on local UAV flights, synthetic city-scale imagery, and long-range aerial sequences. To isolate visual performance, we provide no inertial or GNSS aiding. Performance varies strongly with environment and trajectory scale: MASt3R-SLAM achieves the lowest mean horizontal MAE on the five DJI flights (0.53\% of reference path length), whereas no system consistently preserves global trajectory shape on the long GES and ALTO sequences. Overall, DROID-SLAM performs best, averaging 2.88\% of reference path length across completed runs. Vertical position remains poor, and large-area trajectories remain highly distorted despite loop-closure capability. Current monocular SLAM methods are by themselves therefore insufficient for reliable visual-only aerial navigation.

</details>


### [331] [Progressive Experience Fusion for Multi-Task World Model Control in Endovascular Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18647)
*Harry Robertshaw,Maxence Boels,Nikola Fischer,Sebastien Ourselin,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器，用于自主血管内导航的机械取栓术。PEF在已知解剖结构中的成功率（74%）显著优于Soft Actor-Critic（37%），接近基线TD-MPC2（65%）；在30个血管结构上训练并配合自适应规划视界的PEF控制器，在10个未见血管中达到90%成功率，并成功迁移至体外中风患者血管模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航有望将机械取栓术推广至医疗资源匮乏地区，但控制器需在多变血管解剖结构中导航长距离、多阶段路径。现有控制方法在跨血管结构的泛化能力不足，需要更高效的训练策略来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出渐进式经验融合（PEF）以训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器；设计一种启发式方法，利用残差动作序列离散度动态调整Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）规划视界；并在患者特异性仿真中进行微调以提升跨个体泛化性能。

Result: PEF在五个子任务、十个已知训练解剖结构（保持目标藏出）中平均成功率达74%，优于SAC的37%（p<0.001）和基线TD-MPC2的65%（p=0.053）。自适应规划视界的PEF控制器在30个血管结构上训练后，对10个未见血管达到90%成功率的平均值。在体外中风患者荧光透视血管模型上，微调后路径比从63%提升至80%（p<0.001），仅需4×10⁴步微调（约107分钟临床院间转运时间）。

Conclusion: 该工作为多血管结构训练和患者特异性适应提供了概念验证，证明了PEF结合自适应规划视界与微调策略的有效性，但临床部署前仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous endovascular navigation could support the delivery of mechanical thrombectomy to underserved areas, but controllers must navigate long, multi-stage paths across varying vascular anatomies. This study investigates Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) to train a multi-task TD-MPC2 controller. We additionally evaluate a heuristic that changes the Model Predictive Path Integral planning horizon using residual action-sequence dispersion, and fine-tuning in a patient-specific simulation. Across five subtasks in ten known training anatomies with held-out targets, PEF achieved a mean success rate of 74%, compared with 37% for Soft Actor-Critic (p < 0.001) and 65% for base TD-MPC2 (p = 0.053). A PEF controller with adaptive-horizon planning trained on 30 vasculatures achieved a mean success rate of 90% in ten held-out vasculatures. The PEF agent successfully transferred to an unseen in vitro stroke patient vasculature under fluoroscopy, achieving a mean path ratio improvement from 63% to 80% with fine-tuning (p < 0.001), following 40x103 fine-tuning steps (corresponding to approximately 107 min of clinical inter-hospital transfer time). This work represents a proof of concept for multi-vasculature training and patient-specific adaptation, while further validation is required before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [332] [Orienteering Problem with Uncertain Time-Varying Rewards: Framework and Benchmark for Everyday Service Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18672)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Yuu Jinnai,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出带不确定时变奖励的定向越野问题（OP-UTVR），允许智能体从观测中估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励，通过三种不同规划视野和在线自适应性的规划器求解，并在移动服务机器人基准中验证长视野规划与在线自适应的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向越野问题（OP）大多假设奖励事先已知，但实际应用中奖励往往是不确定且随时间变化的（如配送员的客户需求变化）。这种假设限制了OP在动态环境中的实用性，因此需要一种能够处理不确定时变奖励的新的问题建模方法。

Method: 提出OP-UTVR问题形式化，使智能体能够从观测中估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励。设计了三种规划器，它们在规划视野和在线自适应性上有所区别，并推导了在奖励随机性下三种规划器的理论性能界。同时引入了一个室内移动服务机器人基准环境，机器人在行人中导航。

Result: 实验揭示了规划视野与自适应性之间的权衡关系，并证明了长视野规划结合在线自适应策略的效果最好。

Conclusion: 长视野规划与在线自适应的结合能够有效应对不确定时变奖励环境，为动态环境中定向越野问题的求解提供了理论支撑和实验验证。

Abstract: We present the orienteering problem with uncertain time-varying rewards (OP-UTVR), a novel variant of the orienteering problem (OP). While most existing OP formulations assume rewards to be known in advance, practical applications involve uncertain and time-varying rewards, as with shifting customer demand for delivery agents. OP-UTVR relaxes this assumption by allowing agents to estimate reward dynamics from observations and forecast future rewards. This enables informed routing decisions despite stochastic reward changes and inevitable prediction errors. We address this problem using three planners that differ in planning horizon and online adaptivity, and derive theoretical bounds on their performance under reward stochasticity. We further introduce a mobile service robot benchmark for OP-UTVR, where a robot navigates among pedestrians in indoor environments. Experiments reveal trade-offs between planning horizon and adaptivity, and demonstrate the effectiveness of long-horizon planning with online adaptation.

</details>


### [333] [SoftVTBench: A Deformation-Aware Visuo-Tactile Dataset and Benchmark for Deformable-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18701)
*Bowen Jing,Mingxin Wang,Ruiyang Hao,Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Junjie He,Yang Cui,Yiming Hou,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Dandan Zhang,Ding Zhao,Houde Liu,Xiaofan Li,Si Liu,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftVTBench 是一个面向物理交互感知的可变形物体操作的视觉-触觉数据集与闭环基准，包含 4,000 个专家演示和 50 多个资产（含有限元仿真状态作为评估器专属真值），并定义了变形感知成功率（DSR），用于衡量策略不仅完成任务而且保持变形在容差内。研究发现现有策略的成功轨迹中有 0.7-24% 违反了变形容差，且触觉融合的收益在分布外场景中更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作基准大多只评估任务是否成功，忽视了交互质量（如滑动、过度压缩）。核心瓶颈在于缺乏将策略可见的接触观测与独立物理真值配对的全任务视觉-触觉数据集。

Method: 构建 SoftVTBench 数据集：以 20 Hz 同步多视角 RGB、双指触觉 RGB 与标记运动、本体感知、语言指令、二值与连续夹爪动作，以及仅评估器可见的有限元（FEM）状态。基于固定物体特定标定定义变形感知成功率（DSR），仅在完成任务且峰值归一化变形在容差内时才计为成功。

Result: 在 Diffusion Policy、π_0.5 和 FastWAM 上，所有 12 个分布内配置都有违反变形容差的成功轨迹，占各配置成功率的 0.7-24%。在分布偏移下，视觉-触觉变体在所有六项策略-套件对比中取得更高任务成功率，在五项中取得更高 DSR，但分布内收益不统一。

Conclusion: 触觉信息的可用性本身并不保证有效的多模态融合。SoftVTBench 提供统一的视觉-触觉资源，用于研究策略是否成功、如何与可变形物体物理交互，以及触觉何时能改善这种交互。

Abstract: Physical interaction quality is central to deformable-object manipulation, yet most benchmarks evaluate task success alone. A policy may complete the task while allowing slip or causing excessive compression. A primary bottleneck is the absence of visuo-tactile datasets that pair policy-visible contact observations with independent physical ground truth over complete tasks. We introduce SoftVTBench, a visuo-tactile dataset for physical-interaction-aware deformable-object manipulation. It contains 4,000 expert demonstrations and more than 50 assets, including volumetric deformable objects and visually matched rigid twins. At 20 Hz, each episode synchronizes multi-view RGB, dual-finger tactile RGB and marker motion, proprioception, language, and binary and continuous gripper actions, alongside evaluator-only finite-element (FEM) states. Building upon this dataset, we establish a closed-loop benchmark that uses fixed object-specific calibration to define the Deformation-aware Success Rate (DSR), which counts a rollout as successful only when it completes the task and keeps peak normalized deformation within tolerance.
